Motivation: 大语言模型在分布外泛化任务中表现不佳，核心问题在于任务不敏感：模型倾向于复用训练时的模式而非适应新任务，即使指令被语义破坏或替换为相似任务时仍输出相同动作，且训练过程中注意力从任务标记偏移至局部观察，存在优化偏差。

Method: 提出Task-Perturbed NLL Optimization（任务扰动负对数似然优化），一种轻量级对比正则化器，通过显式约束动作输出与任务指令的关联性，抑制模型对训练模式的过度依赖。

Result: 实验表明该方法有效提升任务敏感性和OOD泛化能力，同时使模型在推理阶段更稳定地关注任务标记，减少注意力漂移现象。

Conclusion: 任务不敏感是大语言模型OOD泛化失效的关键因素，通过引入任务指令依赖的正则化机制可有效缓解该问题，为提升模型鲁棒性提供新思路。

Abstract: Large language models can serve as capable long-horizon agents, but their out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization remains weak. We identify a key source of this failure as task insensitivity: when faced with similar but distinct tasks, models might apply patterns learned during training and fail to solve the task at hand. We show that models often continue with actions aligned with the original task even when the instruction is semantically corrupted and cannot be directly answered. We further find that, when we replace the task description in a trained prompt with another similar but distinct task, the model may still output the same action. This behavior is accompanied by a consistent training-time attention drift away from task tokens and toward local observations, suggesting an optimization bias toward shortcuts. To mitigate this problem, we propose Task-Perturbed NLL Optimization, a lightweight contrastive regularizer that explicitly encourages action dependence on the task instruction. Extensive evaluations show that our intervention improves task sensitivity and OOD generalization while preserving more stable attention to task tokens.

</details>


### [220] [Where Do CoT Training Gains Land in LLM based Agents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26935)
*Jingyu Liu,Zhiwen Wang,Yuxin Jing,Huanyu Zhou,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过对比提示行动与CoT行动，发现CoT训练主要提升了模型直接预测行动的能力，而非增强推理过程的有效性；通过选择性掩码动作监督，可提升域外泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明，语言模型中的CoT推理可能存在事后推理现象，即模型在生成推理前已确定答案。因此需要探究CoT训练是否真正提升模型通过推理改变行动的能力，还是仅增强直接预测行动的能力。

Method: 通过对比不同训练检查点下提示行动（无CoT）与CoT行动（含CoT）的质量，并实施选择性掩码动作监督的训练干预。

Result: 提示行动质量随训练显著提升，但CoT行动相对于提示行动的优势未扩大；后期检查点更少根据CoT修改行动，表明更依赖提示。掩码干预提升了域外泛化能力。

Conclusion: CoT训练主要优化了模型直接预测行动的能力，而非增强推理过程的有效性；通过干预动作监督策略可改善泛化性能。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning is widely used in language-model agents, but prior work has shown that verbalized CoT is not always faithful and may instead reflect post-hoc reasoning, which means the model already knows the answer before reasoning. We therefore ask what CoT training is actually improving: is the model getting better at changing its action through generated reasoning, or is it getting better at predicting the action directly from the prompt? We study this question by comparing \emph{prompt actions} (predicting action without CoT) with CoT actions (predicting action with CoT). Across checkpoints, prompt-action quality improves substantially. While interacting with the environment, the relative advantage of CoT actions over prompt actions remains similar, showing that CoT training does not widen the advantage of CoT reasoning, and it helps to improve the quality of prompt actions. We further find that later checkpoints are less likely to revise the action in response to CoT, suggesting greater reliance on the prompt. Motivated by these patterns, we selectively mask action-token supervision on a fraction of training examples. This intervention improves out-of-domain generalization.

</details>


### [221] [Look-Before-Move: Narrative-Grounded World Visual Attention in Dynamic 3D Story Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26964)
*Jiaming Bian,Bingliang Li,Yuehao Wu,Pichao Wang,Zhi Wang,Hailan Ma,Huadong Mo,Zhenhong Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Look-Before-Move相机规划框架，通过分离观察规范与运动执行，实现动态3D故事世界中叙事驱动的视觉注意力，实验证明该方法在主体感知、意图一致性和轨迹质量方面优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着具身AI和世界模型在动态3D环境中运行，视觉感知需要从被动解释给定观察转向主动决定观察什么。相机规划不仅需生成平滑运动，还需在移动前决定应获取哪些视觉证据。

Method: 提出Look-Before-Move框架，包含三个核心组件：1）语义观察契约（Semantic Observation Contract）将导演意图转换为可执行的视觉约束；2）蒙特卡洛视点搜索（Monte Carlo Viewpoint Search）寻找符合叙事且几何可行的视点；3）语义轨迹接地（Semantic Trajectory Grounding）将选定的视点连接成连续、避碰且时间连贯的相机运动。

Result: 构建了基于StoryBlender的动态3D故事世界基准，包含50个故事、457个场景和1585个镜头，涵盖动画角色、语义场景配置和可执行3D环境。实验表明该框架在主体感知、意图一致性和轨迹质量方面优于代表性基线方法。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在生成相机运动之前组织视觉注意力的重要性，为具身AI在动态3D环境中的主动视觉感知提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: As embodied AI and world models increasingly operate in dynamic 3D environments, visual perception must move beyond passively interpreting given observations toward actively deciding what to observe. We study this problem through camera planning in dynamic 3D story worlds, where the camera must not only generate smooth motion, but also decide what visual evidence should be acquired before it moves. We formulate this capability as Narrative-Grounded World Visual Attention, where the camera acts as an embodied observer that determines what to observe, how to compose the observation, and how to shift attention over time under narrative intent and physical 3D constraints. To realize this capability, we propose Look-Before-Move, a camera planning framework that separates observation specification from motion execution. It first builds a Semantic Observation Contract to convert directorial intent into executable visual constraints, then performs Monte Carlo Viewpoint Search to find narrative-compliant and geometrically feasible viewpoints, and finally applies Semantic Trajectory Grounding to connect selected viewpoints into continuous, collision-aware, and temporally coherent camera motion. We further construct a dynamic 3D Story World Benchmark based on StoryBlender, covering 50 stories, 457 scenes, and 1585 shots with animated characters, semantic scene configurations, and executable 3D environments. Experiments show that our framework improves subject perception, intent consistency, and trajectory quality over representative baselines, demonstrating the importance of organizing visual attention before generating camera motion.

</details>


### [222] [Einstein World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26969)
*Munachiso Samuel Nwadike,Zangir Iklassov,Ali Mekky,Zayd M. Kawakibi Zuhri,Kentaro Inui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Einstein World Models (EWMs)，一种将视觉-时间展开纳入LLM推理过程的新框架，通过生成可检验的视觉假设来增强复杂推理能力，突破纯文本推理局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在复杂推理中受限于纯语言表征，难以处理需超越直接经验的抽象思维；研究探索可视化反事实事件能否作为补充机制提升推理能力。

Method: 设计EWM架构：LLM调用世界模块生成场景的短序列视觉展开，将展开结果作为可检验假设而非最终答案，嵌入推理链中支持后续逻辑推演。

Result: 成功将LLM工具调用能力扩展至视觉思维实验领域，实现文本与视觉假设的协同推理，验证了多模态机制对复杂推理的增益效果。

Conclusion: EWMs证明了视觉化反事实推理可显著增强LLM的抽象思维能力，为构建超越语言局限的通用推理系统提供新范式。

Abstract: Does intelligence require the ability to reason about phenomena beyond direct experience? It is natural to suspect that some complex thought cannot be captured through language alone. However, of particular concern to this work, is whether visualising counterfactual events can complement language as a mechanism for complex thought. We ask whether LLMs can be trained to utilise such visualisation mechanisms, in a way that benefits their reasoning abilities. Motivated by this question, we propose Einstein World Models. EWMs are a blueprint for LLM-based reasoning systems that place visual-temporal rollouts inside the reasoning trace, allowing them to reason in ways that text alone may not support well. In an EWM, the LLM calls a world-module (not to be confused with a world model), to produce short rollouts of scenes under consideration. The returned rollout is treated not as the answer, but as an inspectable hypothesis that can support later reasoning. Einstein World Models extend the capability of LLMs for tool calling (such as web search or code execution), into the domain of visual thought experiments.

</details>


### [223] [Adaptive Utility driven Resource Orchestration for Resilient AI (AURORA-AI)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27005)
*Rahul Umesh Mhapsekar,Ilias Cherkaoui,Lizy Abraham,Indrakshi Dey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AURORA-AI框架，通过自适应资源编排解决非平稳条件下AI系统性能与人本属性（公平性、可解释性）的退化问题，在仿真中显著优于静态基线及其他控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI系统部署在计算、人口统计和运营条件非平稳的环境中，静态资源分配策略会导致预测性能下降，同时损害公平性、可解释性等人本属性，亟需一种能动态适应并维持综合效用的资源编排方法。

Method: 提出AURORA-AI框架，融合Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman反馈控制、基于Lyapunov的稳定性监控和公平感知复合效用，构建单一闭环策略，持续跨异构AI模型群体重分配计算预算，以在扰动下最大化涵盖预测性能、人口统计公平性、成本、延迟、鲁棒性和可解释性的全局效用。

Result: 在包含人口统计偏差冲击、渐进概念漂移和突发黑天鹅事件的离散时间仿真中评估，与静态、轮询、贪婪、LinUCB和PPO等五种控制器对比：AURORA-AI实现黑天鹅事件即时恢复（静态基线需88步，PPO需22步），alpha分位数和超分位数分别提升29%和25%，同时降低平均和最大人口统计公平性差距，并增加Lyapunov稳定运行步骤比例。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于稳定性理论的公平感知自适应编排是迈向鲁棒性人本AI部署的实用且理论驱动的路径。

Abstract: Modern AI systems are increasingly deployed under non-stationary computational, demographic, and operational conditions in which static resource allocation strategies degrade both predictive performance and human-centric properties such as fairness and explainability. This paper presents AURORA-AI, an Adaptive Utility-driven Resource Orchestration framework for Resilient AI that unifies Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman feedback control, Lyapunov-based stability monitoring, and a fairness-aware composite utility into a single closed-loop policy.The framework continuously redistributes computational budget across a population of heterogeneous AI models so that the global utility, defined jointly over predictive performance, demographic parity, cost, latency, robustness, and interpretability, remains maximised under disruption. The framework is evaluated in a stress-rich discrete-time simulation that concurrently injects demographic bias shocks, gradual concept drift, and abrupt black-swan disruptions, and is compared against five established controllers including Static, Round Robin, Greedy, LinUCB, and a deep reinforcement-learning agent based on Proximal Policy Optimisation. AURORA-AI achieves immediate recovery from the black-swan event compared to eighty-eight time steps for the Static baseline and twenty-two for Proximal Policy Optimisation, lifts the alpha-quantile and the super-quantile by twenty-nine and twenty-five percent respectively, simultaneously reduces the mean and maximum demographic parity gap, and increases the fraction of Lyapunov-stable operating steps. These results indicate that fairness-aware adaptive orchestration grounded in stability theory is a practical and theoretically motivated path toward resilient human-centric AI deployment.

</details>


### [224] [Semantic Early-Stopping for Iterative LLM Agent Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27009)
*Sahil Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于语义的早期停止机制，用于优化多智能体LLM循环（如Writer-Critic架构）。通过监测草稿嵌入的语义变化和质量提升趋势，替代传统固定迭代次数终止策略，在保证质量的同时显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有固定迭代次数的终止策略存在两个关键缺陷：1）对简单任务过度消耗token；2）对复杂任务过早截断。这种"语法级开关"无法感知答案质量的实际变化，导致资源分配效率低下。

Method: 提出三重创新方案：1）理论层面证明确定性终止和良好定义性（通过机器验证），将距离序列收敛视为经验性猜想而非Banach收缩映射；2）构建法官高效的评估协议，通过轨迹缓存实现严格配对的成本-质量比较；3）在HotpotQA数据集上进行实证研究，区分操作token与评估token。

Result: 在60题测试集上：1）无法官语义停止器相比max_iterations减少38%操作token（ΔIS=-0.004, p=0.81）；2）完整质量门控变体因单轮评判成本过高反而适得其反；3）理想选择最佳轮次的oracle模型比所有实用策略高+0.115信息得分（p≈4e-11）。

Conclusion: 研究将问题从"何时停止"（已解决）重新定义为"哪一轮最优"（开放挑战）。证明语义感知停止机制可在不损失质量前提下大幅降低计算开销，为多智能体系统提供新的优化范式。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model (LLM) loops, for example a Writer that drafts and a Critic that revises, are almost always terminated by a fixed iteration cap (max_iterations). This is a syntactic kill-switch: it is blind to whether the answer is still improving, so it over-spends tokens on easy inputs and truncates hard ones. We study semantic early-stopping: the loop halts when consecutive draft embeddings stop changing in meaning (cosine distance with a patience window) and the answer's measured quality stops improving. Our work makes three contributions. First, an honest theoretical footing: we prove deterministic termination and well-definedness and machine-check these claims, while treating the convergence of the distance sequence as an empirically tested conjecture rather than a (previously over-claimed) Banach contraction. Second, a judge-efficient evaluation protocol: we generate each question's full trajectory once, replay every stopping policy over the identical drafts, and cache every LLM-judge call, yielding a strictly paired efficiency-versus-quality comparison at low cost; we further separate operational tokens (charged to a policy) from evaluation tokens (a measurement instrument). Third, an empirical study on multi-hop retrieval-augmented question answering (HotpotQA). On the 60-question test split, a judge-free semantic stopper reduces operational tokens by 38% relative to max_iterations at parity quality (Delta-IS = -0.004, p = 0.81), whereas the full quality-gated variant is counter-productive because its per-round judging dominates cost. An oracle that selects the best round attains +0.115 Information Score over every practical policy (p ~ 4e-11), reframing the problem from "when to stop" (easy) to "which round is best" (open).

</details>


### [225] [How to evaluate clustering with ground truth?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27061)
*Pasi Fränti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究评估了聚类算法的外部有效性指标，推荐质心指数（CI）作为直观的簇级度量，并针对点级需求提出PSI和ACC等选项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实标签存在时，需要可靠的外部指标评估聚类性能，尤其关注基于集合匹配的度量方法。

Method: 系统回顾主流外部有效性指标，重点分析集合匹配类方法，对比不同指标的适用场景与特性。

Result: 推荐质心指数（CI）作为可解释的簇级度量；若需点级评估，PSI提供无偏归一化分数，ACC适用于所有点权重相等的场景。

Conclusion: 根据评估粒度需求选择指标：CI适用于簇级解释，PSI解决簇大小偏差问题，ACC满足点级公平性要求。

Abstract: External indexes can be used for cluster evaluation when ground truth is available. We review the most common external validity indexes focusing on set-matching-based measures. We recommend centroid index (CI), because it is an intuitive cluster-level measure with an explainable result. If we need a more fine-tuned, point-level measure, there are more choices. Pair-set index (PSI) provides a normalized score which is not biased by cluster sizes. If all points should matter equally, then clustering accuracy (ACC) or any other set-matching measure is suitable.

</details>


### [226] [Joint Learning of Experiential Rules and Policies for Large Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27136)
*Shicheng Ye,Chao Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出JERP框架，通过联合更新经验规则库与策略模型，解决LLM代理在多步交互环境中经验利用效率低的问题，在AlfWorld和WebShop任务中显著提升决策性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将交互经验分离为外部规则或参数更新：前者易解释但易与策略脱节，后者能全局优化策略但对稀疏奖励下的局部错误修正有限。

Method: JERP通过同一交互轨迹同步更新长期经验规则库和策略模型；决策时检索相关规则并结合交互历史；每轮结束后用轨迹优化策略，并通过对比当前与参考成功轨迹修订规则库。

Result: 在AlfWorld和WebShop复杂交互任务中，JERP显著提升决策性能，验证了规则库与策略协同进化的有效性。

Conclusion: JERP通过耦合机制实现经验规则与策略的同步进化，在保持可解释性的同时增强策略稳定性，为LLM代理的经验复用提供了新范式。

Abstract: For LLM agents in multi-step interactive environments, a key challenge is to make effective use of accumulated interaction experience. Existing work has typically separated two uses of such experience: keeping it outside the model as natural-language rules for later prompting, or using trajectories and feedback to update the model parameters. The former is easy to interpret but can fall out of sync with the evolving policy; the latter improves the policy more broadly but provides only limited correction for local mistakes in sparse-reward settings. We present Joint Learning of Experiential Rules and Policies for LLM Agents (JERP), which updates a long-term experiential-rule pool and the policy from the same interaction trajectories. At decision time, JERP retrieves task-relevant rules and conditions the agent on them together with the interaction history. After each episode, it uses the collected trajectories both to optimize the policy and to revise the rule pool by comparing current rollouts with reference successful trajectories. This coupling keeps the rule pool aligned with the evolving policy while allowing stable and effective behaviors to be gradually absorbed into the model itself. Experiments on AlfWorld and WebShop show that JERP yields consistent gains in decision performance for complex interactive tasks.

</details>


### [227] [OpenRCA 2.0: From Outcome Labels to Causal Process Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27154)
*Aoyang Fang,Yifan Yang,Jin'ao Shang,Qisheng Lu,Junjielung Xu,Rui Wang,Songhan Zhang,Yuzhong Zhang,Boxi Yu,Pinjia He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出PAVE协议解决现有RCA数据集缺乏因果传播路径标注的问题，通过故障注入构建OpenRCA 2.0基准，发现LLM在根因定位上存在显著不足：仅20.7%能准确识别完整根因集合，且多数情况下无法建立可验证的因果链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RCA数据集仅标注根因而忽略因果传播路径，导致任务退化为简单模式匹配，无法全面评估LLM的多步推理和工具使用能力，亟需更严谨的评估框架。

Method: 设计PAVE（逐步标注协议），利用故障注入中的已知干预措施重建因果传播路径，采用前向验证机制（从原因推导效果而非症状反推），构建首个跨系统RCA基准OpenRCA 2.0（500实例）。

Result: 在11个前沿LLM测试中：(1) 仅20.7%案例能准确恢复完整根因集合；(2) 76.0%案例能识别至少一个正确根因服务，但仅61.5%能将其与症状建立可验证的因果路径（即'未验证诊断'现象）。

Conclusion: 结果导向评估会掩盖LLM在因果推理上的根本缺陷，逐步因果标注是构建可信RCA代理的关键缺失要素，需将因果链完整性纳入评估标准。

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) poses a holistic test of LLM agentic capabilities, such as long-context understanding, multi-step reasoning, and tool use. However, existing datasets suffer from a fundamental gap: they label only the root cause, not the propagation path connecting it to the observed symptom, which largely simplifies the task to naive pattern matching. To support rigorous evaluation, we introduce PAVE, a step-wise labeling protocol that leverages known interventions from fault injection to reconstruct causal propagation paths. The mechanism is forward verification: reasoning from cause to effect rather than inferring backward from symptoms. Applying PAVE yields OpenRCA 2.0 (500 instances), the first cross-system RCA benchmark with step-wise causal annotations for LLM agents. Across 11 frontier LLMs, recovering the exact root-cause set succeeds in only 20.7% of cases on average. To locate where this difficulty lies, we relax the criterion and find what we call the ungrounded diagnosis: agents identify at least one correct root-cause service in 76.0% of cases, but ground that service in a verified causal propagation path to the observed symptom in only 61.5%. Outcome-only evaluation hides this failure mode; step-wise causal ground truth is the missing piece for trustworthy LLM-based RCA agents.

</details>


### [228] [TOPS: First-Principles Visual Token Pruning via Constructing Token Optimal Preservation Sets for Efficient MLLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27161)
*Tinghao Wang,Yichen Guo,Rui Huang,Zheng Lu,Qizhe Zhang,Chenxi Li,Yuan Zhang,Jiajun Cao,Zhirong Shen,Yaosong Du,Guangyan Gan,Wenya Wang,Lin William Cong,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TOPS方法，通过信息论分析识别任务相关性、信息覆盖和语义多样性三大原则，实现无需训练的视觉token剪枝，在保持性能的同时大幅减少计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型虽然具备强大的多模态推理能力，但视觉token数量庞大导致计算开销显著。现有视觉token剪枝方法存在不足：基于注意力的方法保留冗余token，基于多样性的方法忽略用户指令，缺乏对token剪枝内在目标的合理公式化。

Method: 从第一性原理角度重新审视视觉token剪枝，将其公式化为构建Token Optimal Preservation Sets。通过自上而下的信息论分析，识别出三个基本原则：任务相关性、信息覆盖和语义多样性。基于这些原则提出TOPS，一个无需训练且与模型无关的剪枝模块。

Result: 在7个MLLM骨干网络和14个基准上的广泛实验表明，TOPS在各种剪枝设置下都优于先前方法。在LLaVA-NeXT上，移除77.8%的视觉token的同时，7B和13B模型分别保持100.0%和100.6%的性能。

Conclusion: 剪枝冗余视觉token有时可以减轻幻觉现象，为未来轻量级MLLM设计提供了重要启发，证明了信息论指导下的token剪枝方法的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved strong multimodal reasoning capabilities, but their efficiency is limited by the large number of visual tokens, which introduces substantial computational overhead. Visual token pruning offers a natural solution, yet existing methods are imperfect: attention-based criteria tend to retain redundant tokens, while diversity-based criteria are often agnostic to user instructions. Even methods that combine multiple criteria still lack a principled formulation of the intrinsic objective of token pruning. In this paper, we revisit visual token pruning from a first-principles perspective and formulate it as constructing Token Optimal Preservation Sets. Through a top-down information-theoretic analysis, we identify three fundamental principles for effective token selection: Task Relevance, Information Coverage, and Semantic Diversity. Based on these principles, we propose TOPS, a training-free and model-agnostic pruning module that can be applied to various MLLMs. Extensive experiments on 7 MLLM backbones and 14 benchmarks demonstrate that TOPS outperforms prior methods under diverse pruning settings. Notably, on LLaVA-NeXT, TOPS removes 77.8% of visual tokens while preserving 100.0% and 100.6% performance on its 7B and 13B models, respectively, suggesting that pruning redundant visual tokens can sometimes mitigate hallucination and inspire future lightweight MLLM design.

</details>


### [229] [A Process Harness for Uplifting Legacy Workflows to Agentic BPM: Design and Realization in CUGA FLO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27188)
*Fabiana Fournier,Lior Limonad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种名为"process harness"的新机制，通过在不替换底层工作流引擎的情况下，将传统工作流升级为智能体业务流程管理(Agentic BPM)。该机制在确定性工作流引擎周围放置策略治理的智能体层，在指定控制点贡献推理、适应性和监督功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作流系统缺乏智能决策能力，难以适应复杂多变的业务环境。传统工作流引擎虽然保证了结构合规性，但无法处理需要推理和适应性的任务。论文旨在解决这一矛盾，既要保持工作流的确定性执行，又要引入智能体的自主决策能力。

Method: 开发了Task-Decision-Flow (TDF)模型，包含三种策略治理的智能体类型：TaskAgent负责知识密集型任务执行，DecisionAgent负责案例级网关路由，FlowAgent通过钩子机制管理运行时流程适应。每种智能体都在来自process FRAME的显式策略内推理。实现了CUGA FLO系统作为TDF模型的具体实现。

Result: 在贷款审批工作流上成功演示了CUGA FLO系统，展示了所有三种智能体类型和基于钩子的监管覆盖功能。系统能够在保持结构合规性的同时，在指定控制点引入策略框架的智能体自主性。

Conclusion: Process harness机制独特地调和了通过确定性工作流执行实现的命令性要求（确保结构合规性）和通过策略框架智能体自主性实现的规范性要求。该方法为传统工作流系统向智能业务流程管理转型提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We introduce the process harness, a new mechanism for uplifting legacy workflows into Agentic Business Process Management (Agentic BPM) without replacing the underlying workflow engine. A process harness places a policy-governed agentic layer around a deterministic workflow engine, intercepting designated control points to contribute reasoning, adaptation, and oversight while the engine retains structural authority over the process. To define the process harness rigorously, we develop the Task-Decision-Flow (TDF) model, specifying both its data schema and its execution semantics. TDF decomposes LLM reasoning across three policy-governed agent types: a TaskAgent for knowledge-intensive task execution, a DecisionAgent for per-case gateway routing, and a FlowAgent that governs runtime flow adaptation through a principled hook mechanism. Each agent reasons within an explicit policy drawn from the process FRAME, the aggregate policy set governing all LLM calls in the system. We then present CUGA FLO as the design and implementation realization of the TDF model, and demonstrate it on a loan approval workflow that exercises all three agent types and hook-driven regulatory override. The process harness uniquely reconciles imperative requirements, realized through deterministic workflow execution that enforces structural compliance, with normative requirements, realized through policy-framed agentic autonomy invoked at designated control points wherever the process demands it.

</details>


### [230] [Vulnerability of Natural Language Classifiers to Evolutionary Generated Adversarial Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27215)
*Manjinder Singh,Alexander E. I. Brownlee,Mohamed Elawady*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GAversary，一种基于遗传算法的黑盒对抗攻击方法，能够显著降低NLP模型准确率，但需要扰动更多词汇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型易受对抗输入攻击，在NLP领域后果严重。现有方法通常需要访问模型内部结构，缺乏有效的黑盒攻击方法。

Method: 提出GAversary混合遗传算法，将目标模型视为黑盒，仅使用logit值指导搜索。使用GloVe嵌入作为变异算子生成词替换，提高对抗样本的语义相似性。

Result: 在多个基准数据集和知名模型上测试，GAversary将目标模型准确率从76.8%降至5.8%（BAE仅降至27.6%）。代价是扰动词汇数量约为其他方法的两倍，语义相似性略低，运行时间增加约5%。

Conclusion: GAversary是一种有效的黑盒对抗攻击方法，能够显著降低NLP模型性能，但需要在攻击效果与文本扰动程度之间进行权衡。

Abstract: Deep learning models have achieved impressive performance across various fields but remain vulnerable to adversarial inputs, particularly in NLP, where such attacks can have significant real-world consequences. Adversarial attacks often involve small, semantically similar token replacements to fool NLP models, and recent methods have become more precise by targeting specific vulnerable words, often by exploiting some level of access to the model's internal structure. This paper proposes GAversary, a hybrid Genetic Algorithm (GA) to generate adversarial attacks on natural language models. The GA is able to treat the target model as a black box, requiring only the logit value output by the model to guide the search. GAversary differs from GAs previously proposed for this problem by using GloVe embeddings to propose word replacements (the mutation operator) to improve the semantic similarity of the adversarial examples. GAversary is applied to several benchmark data sets and well-known target models. GAversary is able to substantially reduce the target model's accuracy on test data compared to the BAE and A2T attacks compared against (in the best case, reducing a 76.8% accuracy to 5.8%, compared to BAE's 27.6%). The trade-off is that GAversary perturbs just under twice as many words as the other two methods, with a slightly lower semantic similarity to the original text and around a 5% increase in run-time.

</details>


### [231] [Ask, Don't Judge: Binary Questions for Interpretable LLM Evaluation and Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27226)
*Sangwoo Cho,Kushal Chawla,Pengshan Cai,Zefang Liu,Chenyang Zhu,Shi-Xiong Zhang,Sambit Sahu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BINEVAL是一个将LLM输出评估标准分解为原子二元问题的框架，通过聚合细粒度问题答案生成可解释的多维评分，解决了传统评估方法不透明、难调试的问题，在多个基准测试中表现优异，并支持提示优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM输出评估是NLP领域的主要瓶颈：人工评估成本高昂且耗时，词汇指标与人类判断相关性差，整体LLM评判者产生不透明的分数难以调试。

Method: BINEVAL框架通过元提示生成细粒度评估问题，LLM独立回答每个输出的问题，将评估标准分解为原子二元问题并聚合结果，产生透明的问题级反馈和校准的整体分数。

Result: 在SummEval、Topical-Chat和QAGS基准上匹配或超越UniEval、G-Eval等强基线，在事实一致性基准QAGS上表现尤为突出，更好匹配人类评分分布，避免天花板效应，支持迭代提示优化。

Conclusion: BINEVAL提供了任务无关、无需训练、可解释的评估框架，结合了强实证性能与实用的诊断优化价值，使评估更易于检查、诊断，并可直接用于提示改进。

Abstract: Evaluating LLM outputs remains a major bottleneck in NLP: human evaluation is expensive and slow, lexical metrics correlate poorly with human judgments on open-ended generation, and holistic LLM judges often produce opaque scores that are hard to debug. We propose BINEVAL, a framework that decomposes evaluation criteria into atomic binary questions and aggregates the resulting verdicts into interpretable, multi-dimensional scores. Given a task prompt, a meta-prompt generates fine-grained evaluation questions, and an LLM answers them independently for each output, yielding transparent question-level feedback together with calibrated overall scores. This decomposition makes evaluation easier to inspect, easier to diagnose, and directly usable for prompt improvement. Across SummEval, Topical-Chat, and QAGS, BINEVAL matches or outperforms strong baselines including UniEval and G-Eval, with especially strong results on factual consistency benchmarks such as QAGS. Beyond competitive correlation with human judgments, BINEVAL better matches human score distributions and avoids the ceiling effects common in prior LLM judges, leading to better discrimination between borderline and clearly flawed outputs. We further show that the same question-level feedback supports iterative prompt optimization, improving evaluator prompts on summarization and generation prompts on IFBench under both self-update and cross-model update settings. Overall, BINEVAL provides a task-agnostic, training-free, and interpretable evaluation framework that combines strong empirical performance with practical diagnostic and optimization value.

</details>


### [232] [EO-WM: A Physically Informed World Model for Probabilistic Earth Observation Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27277)
*Junwei Luo,Shuai Yuan,Zhenya Yang,Yansheng Li,Zhe Liu,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EO-WM，一种基于视频扩散Transformer的多光谱地球观测预测模型，通过物理信息条件框架整合气象强迫信号，解决现有方法在天气响应建模上的不足，并引入两个诊断基准评估极端天气下的植被退化预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地球观测预测方法存在两大局限：1) 确定性模型无法表征预测不确定性；2) 扩散模型未区分气象变量的物理意义，且基准测试仅关注重建精度而非天气响应行为。

Method: EO-WM采用双路径条件框架：1) 分离气候基准与天气异常信号；2) 累积异常强迫时序建模持续热/干旱应力。结合视频扩散Transformer架构，实现多光谱EO时序预测。

Result: 实验显示EO-WM在NDVI下降幅度预测误差降低5.63%，方向命中率提升7.80%，同时在标准像素级指标保持竞争力。两个新基准验证了模型对极端天气和季节变化的响应能力。

Conclusion: 首次将物理机制融入EO预测的条件建模，通过诊断基准突破传统评估范式，为气象响应型地球系统建模提供新范式。开源代码与基准将促进领域发展。

Abstract: Earth Observation (EO) forecasting aims to predict future Earth surface dynamics from satellite observations under changing meteorological conditions. In this paper, we view this task as a partially observed, weather-driven world modeling problem, in which weather acts as a conditioning signal, while forecasting remains uncertain due to sparse observations and unobserved land-surface states. However, existing methods do not fully capture this setting: deterministic models collapse uncertainty into a single future prediction, while diffusion-based methods typically treat weather variables as undifferentiated conditioning signals, and existing benchmarks focus mainly on reconstruction accuracy rather than whether forecasts respond correctly to changed weather forcing.We introduce EO-WM, a video diffusion transformer for multispectral EO forecasting. EO-WM incorporates a physically informed conditioning framework that represents meteorological forcing through a climatological baseline, weather anomalies, and cumulative physical stress signals. Specifically, it separates baseline and anomaly through distinct conditioning pathways, and accumulates anomalous forcing over time to capture sustained heat and drought stress. To evaluate weather-response behavior beyond standard metrics, we introduce two diagnostic benchmarks: an Extreme Summer Benchmark for severity-aware prediction of vegetation degradation under extreme weather, and a Seasonal Matched-Pair Benchmark for testing response fidelity under changed weather forcing. Experiments show that EO-WM reduces the error in predicted Normalized Difference Vegetation Index (NDVI) decline amplitude by a relative 5.63% and improves directional hit rate by a relative 7.80%, while remaining competitive on standard pixel-level metrics. The benchmarks and model will be made open-source at https://github.com/Luo-Z13/EO-WM.

</details>


### [233] [Simulation-based inference for rapid Bayesian parameter estimation in epidemiological models: a comparison with MCMC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27286)
*Alina Bazarova,Johann Fredrik Jadebeck,Henrik Zunker,Carolina J. Klett-Tammen,Torben Heinsohn,Wolfgang Wiechert,Katharina Noeh,Stefan Kesselheim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出使用基于模拟的推断（SBI）替代传统MCMC方法校准机械流行病学模型，在COVID-19 ICU数据上验证了其高效性和准确性，计算速度提升10倍以上，适用于实时疫情分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯校准方法MCMC在高维非线性系统和重复近实时分析中计算成本过高，亟需可扩展的替代方案。

Method: 采用神经后验估计的SBI方法校准SECIR流行病学模型，使用德国2020年ICU占用数据，通过31天和201天两种时间窗口对比SBI与MCMC的后验分布一致性及计算效率。

Result: 31天窗口下SBI后验分布与MCMC高度一致且准确预测ICU轨迹；201天复杂场景下保持主导后验结构；计算耗时显著降低（31天问题60-70秒 vs 1000秒，201天问题157秒 vs 19000秒）。

Conclusion: SBI为机械流行病学模型的贝叶斯校准提供了快速高效的框架，支持重复近实时推断和快速疫情分析，具有显著计算优势。

Abstract: Mechanistic epidemiological models are widely used to support infectious disease forecasting and public-health decision making. Bayesian calibration of such models is commonly performed using Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC), which can become computationally expensive for high-dimensional nonlinear systems and repeated near-real-time analyses. Here, we investigate simulation-based inference (SBI) using neural posterior estimation as a scalable alternative for Bayesian calibration of a mechanistic SECIR epidemiological model using COVID-19 intensive care unit (ICU) occupancy data from Germany during 2020. We compared SBI and MCMC across multiple epidemic phases using both 31-day inference windows and a substantially more challenging 201-day reconstruction problem involving multiple transmission change points. Posterior agreement was evaluated quantitatively using Wasserstein distances and Kullback-Leibler divergences together with posterior predictive checks. Across the 31-day windows, SBI recovered posterior distributions in strong agreement with MCMC while accurately reproducing observed ICU trajectories. In the 201-day setting, SBI preserved the dominant posterior structure despite increased uncertainty. SBI, by combining CPU and GPU resources, substantially reduced computational runtime compared with MCMC, which was restricted to running on CPUs. Whereas MCMC required approximately 1000 seconds for the 31-day inference problems, SBI achieved comparable posterior and predictive performance in approximately 60-70 seconds on a single GPU. For the 201-day inference problem, SBI required an average of 157 seconds, while the MCMC runs took over 19,000 seconds. Our results demonstrate that SBI provides a rapid and computationally efficient framework for Bayesian calibration of mechanistic epidemiological models, supporting repeated near-real-time inference and rapid outbreak analysis.

</details>


### [234] [Prompt Injection in Automated Résumé Screening with Large Language Models: Single and Multi-Injection Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27287)
*Preet Baxi,Jiannan Xu,Jane Yi Jiang,Stefanus Jasin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究了在自动简历筛选中，候选人通过提示注入（即添加自我宣传文本但不增加新资格）来操纵LLM评估的现象。实验表明，当简历质量同质且少数候选人注入时，提示注入能有效提升排名，但随着更多人注入，效果迅速下降；在候选人质量异质时，注入效果平均较低，但偶尔会让低质量候选人超越高质量候选人，引发公平性问题。LLM筛选在操纵罕见且候选人质量差异较小时最脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs越来越多地用于筛选和排名求职者，这激励候选人策略性地操纵算法招聘系统。因此，研究提示注入在自动简历筛选中的影响。

Method: 使用受控实验来研究提示注入的效果。

Result: 当简历质量同质且少数候选人注入时，提示注入可靠地提升申请人排名；随着更多候选人注入，其有效性迅速下降，当操纵变得普遍时崩溃；当候选人质量异质时，提示注入平均效果较低，但偶尔允许低质量候选人超越高质量候选人。

Conclusion: LLM-based screening在操纵罕见且候选人质量差异较小时最脆弱；提示注入可能引发公平性问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to screen and rank job applicants, creating incentives for candidates to strategically manipulate algorithmic hiring systems. We study prompt injection in automated résumé screening, defined as subtle self-promotional text that introduces no new qualifications but is designed to influence LLM evaluations. Using controlled experiments, we show that prompt injection reliably improves applicant rankings when résumé quality is homogeneous and few candidates inject. However, its effectiveness rapidly diminishes as more candidates inject, collapsing when manipulation becomes widespread. When candidate quality is heterogeneous, prompt injection is less effective on average, but can occasionally allow lower-quality candidates to outrank higher-quality ones, raising fairness concerns. Overall, LLM-based screening is most vulnerable when manipulation is rare and candidate quality differences are small. Code and resources are publicly available at: https://github.com/preetb1199/Prompt_Injection_ACL26

</details>


### [235] [When Does Combining Language Models Help? A Co-Failure Ceiling on Routing, Voting, and Mixture-of-Agents Across 67 Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27288)
*Josef Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多模型LLM系统的性能增益存在理论上限，由所有模型同时出错的比例(β)决定。研究发现传统相关性指标无法准确预测β，实际β值比模型预测高2-5倍。性能提升关键在于模型在不同问题上的失败差异，而非简单增加模型数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模型LLM系统（路由、投票、级联、融合、混合代理）被广泛用来超越单模型准确性，但领域内很少报告其性能增益的上限，需要理解这些系统性能提升的理论边界和实际限制。

Method: 使用Clopper-Pearson边界对β进行有限样本估计；跨67个模型（来自21个提供商）进行大规模实验；采用tetrachoric校准的单因素模型和高斯copula模型分析错误相关性；在开放数学、代码执行、GPQA-Diamond等任务上进行对比实验；使用5人评审小组（kappa 0.73-0.92）评估自由回答形式。

Result: 1) 任何输出为成员模型答案的策略，其准确性不能超过1-β；2) 在开放数学任务中，观察β=0.052，而70模型高斯copula预测β=0.023，低估约2.5倍（90%CI: 1.7-3.4）；3) 代码执行任务β=0.079；4) GPQA-Diamond自由回答形式β=0.127；5) 低ρ异构集成优于高ρ Self-MoA；6) 在可检查任务中，组合模型很少超越单最佳模型。

Conclusion: 多模型LLM系统的性能增益受限于所有模型在同一查询上同时出错的比例(β)，而非简单的模型数量增加。在可检查任务中，组合模型很少能超越单最佳模型，除非有强查询级路由信号。性能提升来源于模型在不同问题上的失败模式差异，而非简单地添加更多模型。

Abstract: Multi-model LLM systems such as routing, voting, cascades, fusion, and mixture-of-agents are used to beat single-model accuracy. We show that their gain is capped by a quantity the field rarely reports. For any policy whose output is one member model answer, accuracy cannot exceed one minus beta, where beta is the rate at which every model is wrong on the same query. In contrast, the usual diagnostic, average pairwise error correlation rho, cannot identify beta: error laws with identical marginals and pairwise correlations can have different all-wrong rates. A Clopper-Pearson bound on beta gives a finite-sample certificate on the largest gain any router, vote, or cascade could deliver before training a router.
  Across 67 models from 21 providers, a tetrachoric-calibrated single-factor model still underprices the all-wrong tail: on open-ended mathematics, observed beta is 0.052 versus 0.023 under the full 67-model Gaussian copula, about 2.5 times underpricing, with 90 percent CI 1.7 to 3.4 and k equals 17. The effect recurs on execution-graded code, where beta is 0.079. Re-asking the same GPQA-Diamond questions in free-response rather than multiple-choice form reopens the tail, with beta 0.127 and a five-judge panel with kappa 0.73 to 0.92, locating co-failure in answer format rather than subject. At matched quality, low-rho heterogeneous ensembles beat high-rho Self-MoA, but on checkable tasks in our pool, combining models rarely beats the single best model without a strong query-level routing signal. Gains come from models failing on different questions, not from adding more models.

</details>


### [236] [Language-Based Digital Twins for Elderly Cognitive Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27334)
*Mohammad Mehdi Hosseini,Mohammad H. Mahoor,Hiroko H. Dodge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于大语言模型的语言数字孪生框架，用于模拟老年人对话行为，实现认知健康早期监测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生在个性化医疗中前景广阔，但认知健康领域轻度认知障碍(MCI)早期检测仍具挑战，语言和对话模式可作为非侵入性生物标志物

Method: 构建基于大语言模型的语言数字孪生框架，结合文体学线索和上下文元数据模拟老年人对话行为；引入多头条件变分自编码器(cVAE)联合评估重建质量和预测认知评分

Result: 在I-CONECT数据集上验证，数字孪生保持身份特定特征，重建和MoCA预测误差与真实数据相当，优于基线GPT生成响应

Conclusion: 语言基础数字孪生作为可扩展、非侵入性方法，在个性化和连续认知健康监测方面展现出巨大潜力

Abstract: Digital twins have emerged as a promising paradigm for personalized healthcare, enabling modeling of individual behavior and health trajectories. In cognitive health, early detection of Mild Cognitive Impairment (MCI) remains challenging, where language and conversational patterns serve as non-invasive biomarkers. In this work, we propose a language-based digital twin framework that leverages large language models (LLMs) to mimic the conversational behavior of elderly individuals by incorporating stylometric cues and contextual metadata. To evaluate fidelity and cognitive consistency, we introduce a multi-head conditional variational autoencoder (cVAE) that jointly measures reconstruction quality and predicts cognitive scores. Experiments on the I-CONECT dataset show that the digital twin preserves identity-specific characteristics and achieves reconstruction and MoCA prediction errors comparable to real data, while outperforming baseline GPT-generated responses. These results highlight the potential of language-based digital twins as a scalable and non-invasive approach for personalized and continuous cognitive health monitoring.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [237] [Machine Learning-Driven Content Popularity Prediction and Cache Optimization in D2D Clustered Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26119)
*Maede Rezaei,Ahmadreza Montazerolghaem*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于机器学习的缓存优化框架，通过动态预测用户和集群级内容需求，结合用户参与意愿优化D2D网络中的缓存放置，显著提升缓存利用率和命中率，有效缓解蜂窝网络后链路压力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能设备普及，高质量内容需求激增导致蜂窝网络后链路拥堵，传统缓存策略未充分考虑用户偏好差异（如80%流量由20%用户产生），亟需智能内容选择方案。

Method: 设计ML CPCO框架：1）利用机器学习算法预测用户/集群级内容请求；2）评估集群级内容热度；3）结合用户缓存参与意愿优化缓存放置策略；4）通过D2D通信实现高效内容分发。

Result: 仿真验证显示：1）缓存利用率达97%（对比方法最高）；2）命中率显著提升；3）执行时间可控；4）有效降低后链路流量并改善用户体验。

Conclusion: 所提框架通过动态需求预测与用户异质性建模，在D2D集群网络中实现了高效缓存优化，为缓解蜂窝网络后链路压力提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: Advancements in wireless communication technology have led to the widespread use of smart devices including computers, mobile phones, tablets, wearable devices, and vehicles which has significantly increased the demand for high quality content. This growing demand puts pressure on the backhaul links in cellular networks, resulting in congestion and content delivery delays. To address this, cache enabled networks and edge caching, such as caching in user devices, have emerged as promising solutions to reduce backhaul traffic. By caching content locally and using device to device (D2D) communication for retrieval, content delivery can be made more efficient. However, limited cache capacity requires intelligent content selection strategies. The popularity of the content is dynamic and varies with user preferences, where less than 20% of the users generate 80% of multimedia traffic. Many existing methods fail to consider this user heterogeneity, often assuming uniform preferences throughout the network. This paper proposes a novel Machine Learning Driven Content Popularity Prediction and Cache Optimization (ML CPCO) framework that dynamically predicts user and cluster level content demand, incorporates user willingness to participate in caching, and optimizes cache placement in D2D enabled clustered networks. The system predicts future content requests using machine learning algorithms and estimates content popularity at the cluster level. Based on these predictions, cache placement decisions are made to maximize efficiency. The simulation results show that the proposed approach performs well under various network conditions, achieving a cache utilization rate of nearly 97% the highest among the methods compared. In addition, it offers an improved hit rate with an acceptable execution time, resulting in reduced backhaul traffic and enhanced user experience.

</details>


### [238] [Dot-Flik: A Scalable Edge AI Architecture for Distributed Insect Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26121)
*Mattia Consani,Denisa-Andreea Constantinescu,Åse Håtveit,Titus Venverloo,Fabio Duarte,Carlo Ratti,David Atienza*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该研究针对昆虫种群监测的高成本和高能耗问题，提出边缘计算帧过滤算法、分布式IoT架构及实地验证方案，实现60-80%帧数削减、22.6%节能和5-6路并发处理能力，为城市生物多样性监测提供低成本可扩展解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有昆虫视觉监测系统存在硬件成本高、能耗大、依赖集中处理或云连接的问题，难以实现大规模部署。

Method: 1. 基于时间差分、伽马校正运动增强和块运动密度分析的运动感知帧过滤算法；2. 解耦数据采集与AI分类的分布式分层IoT架构；3. 在低成本硬件上进行实时性能、网络扩展性、硬件成本和风环境下的能耗验证。

Result: 轻风条件下实现60-80%帧数削减，维持30FPS实时处理（计算余量12.8ms），节能22.6%，单中心节点支持5-6路边缘流并发。

Conclusion: 验证了低成本、可扩展的昆虫监测网络可行性，为城市环境生物多样性监测提供实践基础。

Abstract: Global insect population declines necessitate scalable, continuous monitoring systems, yet existing vision-based solutions remain constrained by high hardware costs, energy demands, and reliance on centralized processing or cloud connectivity. This article presents three contributions to address these limitations. First, we propose a motion-informed frame filtering algorithm based on temporal differencing, gamma-corrected motion amplification, and block-based motion density analysis that discards irrelevant frames at the edge while preserving insect activity, without requiring deep learning inference on the sensing device. Second, we introduce a distributed, hierarchical IoT architecture that decouples data acquisition from AI classification through this edge-level preprocessing, projecting fractional scaling of central processing requirements and significantly increasing monitoring coverage compared to monolithic single-stream approaches. Third, we validate the complete system through real-world outdoor deployments on low-cost commodity hardware along four axes: real-time performance, network scalability, hardware cost, and energy efficiency under varying wind conditions. Results demonstrate 60-80% frame reduction under light-wind conditions, sustained real-time 30 FPS operation with 12.8 ms of computational headroom, up to 22.6% energy savings, and support for 5-6 concurrent edge streams per central node. These findings establish a practical foundation for dense, low-cost biodiversity monitoring networks in urban environments.

</details>


### [239] [Privacy-Aware Agent Collaboration for Dynamic VR Slice Management in 6G SD-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26123)
*Khaled M. Naguib,Soumaya Cherkaoui,Mahmoud M. Elmesalawy,Ahmed M. Abd El-Haleem,Ibrahim I. Ibrahim*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 针对6G网络中VR服务对超低延迟和高吞吐量的需求，提出一种基于移动性预测和隐私保护的多智能体强化学习框架，实现VR切片的高效资源管理，在仿真实验中显著提升性能并降低隐私风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G网络中的VR服务要求超低延迟和高吞吐量，这对SD-RAN的动态资源管理构成挑战，现有方法难以平衡资源效率与用户隐私保护。

Method: 设计移动性驱动的隐私感知多智能体强化学习框架，通过移动性预测优化资源分配，结合信息瓶颈编码器实现安全智能体协作，保障端到端VR链路性能与数据隐私。

Result: 仿真实验显示：相比传统方法，吞吐量提升34%，资源消耗减少28%，隐私泄露降低85%。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了6G环境下VR服务的资源管理难题，在保障隐私的前提下显著提升系统性能，为未来沉浸式VR应用提供可靠技术支撑。

Abstract: Ultra-low latency and high throughput are required for Virtual Reality (VR) services in 6G networks, which presents critical challenges for Software-Defined Radio Access Networks (SD-RANs) dynamic resource management. This work propose a mobility-driven, privacy-aware Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) framework for VR slice management, in which cooperative agents maximize resource distribution over end-to-end VR links while protecting the privacy of user data. Our approach incorporates mobility prediction and an information bottleneck encoder to facilitate effective and secure agent collaboration. In simulations, comparisons with traditional methods are studied which show up to 34\% throughput improvement, 28\% fewer resources, and 85\% less privacy leakage, guaranteeing dependable immersive VR experiences in future 6G environments.

</details>


### [240] [Enhancing FANET Routing Resilience: A Fuzzy-Driven Bio-Inspired Approach and Its Quantitative Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26124)
*Xinwang Yuan,Jinshu Su,Yusheng Xia,Congxi Song*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 针对无人机自组织网络高动态特性，提出改进的簇头选举算法与自适应控制机制，显著提升网络稳定性与效率，同时揭示峰值性能与参数稳定性的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机自组织网络中，高移动性导致拓扑快速变化和链路不稳定，传统聚类方法难以维持稳定簇结构，且控制开销随hello间隔缩短急剧增加，亟需优化簇头选举、簇维护机制及控制开销管理。

Method: 1. 多因素优化改进人工蜂群簇头选举算法；2. 双层信标机制实现动态簇维护；3. 模糊逻辑控制器自适应调整hello间隔；4. 提出参数敏感性指数量化路由韧性评估。

Result: NS-3仿真显示：所提协议在吞吐量、延迟等指标显著优于基线方案，参数敏感性分析揭示其高动态环境适应性，但峰值性能与参数稳定性存在固有矛盾。

Conclusion: 该研究通过多技术融合有效提升了FANETs的簇稳定性和资源效率，所构建的韧性评估框架为动态网络协议设计提供了量化分析工具，但需进一步优化性能与稳定性的平衡策略。

Abstract: In flying ad hoc networks, the high mobility of unmanned aerial vehicles leads to rapid topology changes and unstable links. Clustering can simplify topology management, yet maintaining stable cluster structures remains challenging in highly dynamic environments. This paper employs multi-factor optimization to enhance the artificial bee colony-based cluster head election algorithm and introduces a dual-layer beacon mechanism for cluster maintenance, thereby improving cluster stability and reducing reconfiguration overhead. Meanwhile, although shortening the hello interval helps capture link variations in a timely manner, it significantly increases control overhead. To address this issue, a fuzzy logic-based hello interval controller is proposed to adaptively adjust control message frequency according to network dynamics, effectively reducing overhead while preserving transmission performance. Furthermore, to quantitatively evaluate routing resilience in large-scale and highly dynamic scenarios, this paper proposes a parameter sensitivity index to characterize both overall protocol performance and its sensitivity to environmental variations. The proposed protocol is compared with common baseline schemes in NS-3 simulations, and the resilience evaluation framework analysis reveals significant advantages in overall performance while exposing the trade-off between peak performance and parametric stability.

</details>


### [241] [Geometric Fairness-Aware Routing for Federated Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26125)
*Ratun Rahman*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: Geo-FairFed是一个结合双曲图神经网络和联邦优化的几何公平感知路由系统，用于6G和边缘智能网络，能显著降低延迟、能耗并提升公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦路由系统通常优先考虑总延迟或吞吐量，而忽略了公平性和网络拓扑的几何结构。新兴的6G和边缘智能网络需要有效的、平衡的路由算法来应对多样化且空间分布的设备。

Method: 使用双曲图神经网络(HGNNs)在负曲率流形上学习拓扑感知表示，包含层次关系和连接不对称性；结合联邦优化，全局聚合器使用曲率正则化目标函数，最小化路由损失、几何不一致性和基于Jain公平指数的不平等惩罚。

Result: 在动态6G边缘和IoT拓扑上的广泛仿真显示：平均延迟降低20%，能耗减少17%，公平性提升最多21%。理论分析证明在有限曲率下具有收敛保证，提出的公平项导致路由性能的帕累托改进均衡。

Conclusion: 将网络拓扑嵌入双曲流形并将公平性纳入联邦更新可以显著增强大规模网络路由的效率和公平性，为6G和边缘智能网络提供了有效的路由解决方案。

Abstract: Emerging 6G and edge-intelligent networks require effective and balanced routing algorithms among varied and spatially distributed devices. Existing federated routing systems often prioritize aggregate latency or throughput above fairness and the underlying geometric structure of network topologies. This paper describes Geo-FairFed, a geometric fairness-aware routing system that blends hyperbolic graph neural networks (HGNNs) and federated optimization to provide equal performance across edge nodes. Each node learns topology-aware representations on a negatively curved manifold, which include hierarchical relationships and connection asymmetries. A global aggregator next enforces fairness using a curvature-regularized aim that minimizes routing loss, geometric inconsistency, and an inequality penalty based on Jain's fairness index. A theoretical analysis develops convergence guarantees under limited curvature and shows that the proposed fairness term results in a Pareto-improving equilibrium in routing performance. Extensive simulations on dynamic 6G-edge and IoT topologies reveal that Geo-FairFed minimizes average latency by 20\%, reduces energy consumption by 17\%, and improves fairness by up to 21\% when compared to state-of-the-art federated and geometric routing protocols. The study found that embedding topology in a hyperbolic manifold and including fairness into federated updates can significantly enhance the efficiency and equity of large-scale network routing.

</details>


### [242] [HALO: Hierarchical Auction-assisted Learning for Offloading in SAGIN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26293)
*Xuli Cai,Poonam Lohan,Sachin Ravikant Trankatwar,Burak Kantarci*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出HALO框架，通过分层拍卖机制与HPPO算法解决SAGIN网络中延迟敏感的任务卸载与资源调度问题，在任务成功率上显著优于现有DRL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对三层天地一体化网络（SAGIN）中IoT设备、无人机边缘节点与高空平台站构成的复杂环境，解决延迟敏感任务在动态资源约束下的联合调度难题。现有方法难以平衡计算效率与通信资源分配，且缺乏对细粒度系统动态的建模能力。

Method: 1. 构建非凸混合整数非线性规划（MINLP）模型，联合优化任务关联与连续资源控制（带宽/功率/CPU频率）
2. 设计宏微时隙模型追踪传输/计算累积进度
3. 提出HALO框架：采用拍卖机制处理任务关联，结合分层近端策略优化（HPPO）实现资源分配

Result: 仿真验证显示：
- 相比PPO提升任务成功率8.7个百分点（相对增益11.4%）
- 比DDPG/SAC鲁棒性分别提升32.4%/89.9%
- 在不同流量负载下保持性能稳定性

Conclusion: HALO通过分层决策机制有效解决了SAGIN网络的NP-hard调度问题，在延迟敏感场景下展现出显著优于传统DRL方法的性能与鲁棒性，为天地一体化网络资源管理提供新思路。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate delay-aware task offloading and resource scheduling in a three-tier space-air-ground integrated network (SAGIN) consisting of IoT devices, UAV edge nodes, and a high-altitude platform station (HAPS). We formulate joint task association and continuous resource control (including bandwidth, transmit power, and CPU frequency allocation) as a non-convex mixed-integer nonlinear programming (MINLP) problem, which is inherently NP-hard. To capture fine-grained system dynamics, we introduce a macro-micro slot model that tracks cumulative transmission and computation progress over time. Based on this model, we propose HALO, a hierarchical auction-assisted learning framework that combines auction-based task association with hierarchical Proximal Policy Optimization (HPPO) for resource allocation. Simulation results under different traffic loads show that HALO consistently outperforms representative deep reinforcement learning (DRL) baselines. In particular, HALO achieves an average improvement of 8.7 percentage points in task success rate over PPO (corresponding to an 11.4% relative gain) and shows consistently greater robustness than DDPG and SAC, with relative improvements of 32.4% and 89.9%, respectively. These results highlight HALO's ability to maintain stable and efficient performance under varying traffic conditions, making it well-suited for delay-sensitive SAGIN environments.

</details>


### [243] [5G NR-V2X Scheduling Approaches for CPM Variable Size Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26746)
*Vittorio Todisco,Mattia Andreani,Maria Luisa Merani,Alessandro Bazzi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了5G NR-V2X Mode 2中动态调度与多种SPS策略在真实CPM流量下的性能，发现资源分配稳定性比瞬时负载优化更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SPS调度机制针对稳定包大小的周期性流量设计，但真实CPM流量存在显著包大小波动，其对分布式资源分配的影响尚未充分理解。

Method: 基于真实感知数据集重建变大小CPM流量，系统级对比分析动态调度、填充式SPS、激进重选SPS及MCS自适应策略的性能表现。

Result: 包大小波动会显著降低可靠性；动态调度和激进重选因频繁资源重分配导致碰撞概率上升；填充式SPS实现稳定资源分配；MCS自适应平均接收率最高但不同包类型可靠性不均衡。

Conclusion: 在真实CPM流量下，资源使用稳定性优先于瞬时负载优化，为NR-V2X Mode 2上的CPS部署提供设计指导。

Abstract: Collective perception messages (CPMs) introduce significant packet size variability due to dynamic object inclusion and periodic security overhead. While 5G NR-V2X Mode 2 typically employs semi-persistent scheduling (SPS) designed for periodic traffic with relatively stable packet sizes, the impact of realistic CPM-driven size fluctuations on distributed resource allocation remains insufficiently understood. This paper presents a comparative system-level evaluation of NR-V2X Mode 2 scheduling strategies under variable-size CPM traffic reconstructed from real-world perception datasets. We analyze dynamic scheduling and multiple SPS-based approaches, including padding-based allocation, aggressive size-driven reselection, and modulation and coding scheme (MCS) adaptation. Results show that packet size variability can significantly degrade reliability when scheduling stability is compromised. In particular, dynamic scheduling and aggressive reselection increase collision probability due to frequent resource reallocations. In contrast, SPS with padding converges to a stable resource allocation and provides robust performance, while MCS adaptation achieves the highest average packet reception ratio but with uneven reliability across packet types. The findings demonstrate that, under realistic CPM traffic, stability of resource usage is more critical than instantaneous load optimization, and provide design guidelines for CPS deployment over NR-V2X Mode 2.

</details>


### [244] [An Experimental Assessment of the Spatial and Frequency Selectivity of Reconfigurable Intelligent Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26808)
*Cyrille Morin,Leonardo S Cardoso,Maxime Guillaud,Ahmad Shokair,Youssef Nasser,Amelie Hennequart,Geoffroy Lerosey*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究通过实验验证了可重构智能表面（RIS）对非目标通信链路的显著影响，发现即使频率分离也无法缓解该效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究RIS在优化主通信链路配置时，对邻近其他无线链路（同频或异频）的潜在干扰问题，这对多用户共享频谱环境下的RIS部署至关重要。

Method: 在FR1频段使用CorteXlab无线电测试平台与Greenerwave RIS设备，针对三种典型场景（同频/异频、不同空间位置）进行实验分析。

Result: 实验表明RIS配置会显著改变次要链路的接收功率与信道相位特性，且该影响在超出RIS标称工作频段时依然存在，载波频率分离无法有效抑制此现象。

Conclusion: RIS的部署需考虑其对非目标链路的副作用，传统频率隔离方案可能不足以应对此类干扰，未来系统设计应纳入跨链路影响评估机制。

Abstract: This work investigates the impact of reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RIS) on radio links other than the one for which the RIS configuration is optimized. We consider three different scenarios in which a secondary communication link could be affected by a RIS whose configuration is optimized for a primary communication link operating in the vicinity, on the same or on different frequencies. This question is investigated experimentally in the FR1 band, using the CorteXlab radio testbed and a Greenerwave RIS. We show that the impact, in terms of received power and impact on the channel phase of the secondary link, is significant even outside of the nominal frequency range of the RIS, and is not mitigated by carrier frequency separation between the two communication links.

</details>


### [245] [Toward Agentic SysAdmin: Rethinking System Administration with AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26960)
*Gianmaria Frigo,Davide Saladino,Alberto Castagnaro,Francesco Marchiori,Denis Donadel,Luca Pajola,Mauro Conti*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: NetLLMeval框架通过实时网络模拟自动评估LLM在网络管理任务中的表现，实验显示优化求解器可大幅提升模型准确性，开源支持复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM网络管理评估方法依赖静态参考输出或人工验证，无法应对网络状态多样性及不同部署策略（如单体提示到代理流水线），亟需可扩展的自动评估框架。

Method: 利用实时网络模拟生成无干预基准，通过24000次全因子实验（10个基础模型、4种求解器架构、10类任务、6种拓扑）系统评估LLM性能。

Result: 求解器设计对准确性影响显著（14B开源模型正确率从0.43提升至0.88），本地部署模型在优化配置下可匹敌万亿参数前沿系统。

Conclusion: NetLLMeval为LLM网络管理提供可复现的评估标准，推动自动化网络管理技术发展，框架已开源以促进未来研究。

Abstract: The growing complexity of computer networks, driven by cloud-native architectures, heterogeneous devices, and distributed systems, places increasing pressure on network administrators who must simultaneously manage configuration, troubleshooting, and security under tight operational constraints. Large Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as a promising tool to assist and partially automate these tasks, yet their systematic evaluation in networking scenarios remains an open challenge. Existing benchmarks rely on static reference outputs or manual expert validation, neither of which scales to the diversity of real-world network states or to the variety of orchestration strategies -- from monolithic prompting to fully agentic pipelines --through which LLMs are increasingly deployed.
  In this paper, we present NetLLMeval, a framework for automatically evaluating LLM-based systems on network administration tasks by leveraging live network emulation to derive ground truth without human intervention. Through a full-factorial study of 24000 runs spanning 10 foundation models, 4 solver architectures, 10 task types, and 6 network topologies of increasing complexity, we show that solver design has a great impact on accuracy -- lifting a 14B open-weight model from 0.43 to 0.88 correctness -- and that such locally-deployable models can match trillion-parameter frontier systems under the right configuration. NetLLMeval is released open-source to support reproducible benchmarking of future models and solver designs.

</details>


### [246] [Maximum Achievable Burst Size in All-Optical Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27050)
*Thomas Roethig,Sleman Mouammar,Ítalo Brasileiro,Soheil Hosseini,Admela Jukan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究分析了不同轨道高度LEO星座在全光卫星网络中的最大突发传输能力，发现低轨LEO比高轨LEO支持更大的突发包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化全光卫星网络的突发传输性能，探索不同轨道高度对网络容量的影响。

Method: 基于WDM技术构建光突发交换网络模型，在100 Gbps上行容量条件下进行仿真分析。

Result: 高轨LEO星座支持最大500 MB突发包，低轨LEO星座支持最大600 MB突发包。

Conclusion: 低轨LEO星座具有更优的突发传输能力，为未来卫星网络设计提供关键参数参考。

Abstract: We analyze the maximum burst size achievable in all-optical satellite networks across different constellations. With a 100 Gbps uplink capacity, a WDM-based optical burst switching network supports burst sizes of up to 500 MB in high-altitude LEO constellations and 600 MB in low-altitude LEO constellations.

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [247] [Tracking the Turn: Mamba-Powered Human Orientation Detection using UWB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26187)
*Mohammad Cheraghinia,Adnan Shahid,Jaron Fontaine,Cedric De Cock,David Plets,Eli De Poorter*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种纯UWB方案，通过双向Mamba架构和卡尔曼滤波后处理，直接从UWB信道脉冲响应中预测人体偏航方向，显著降低误差并优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室内定位系统多聚焦位置估计，身体方向依赖次级设备（如IMU），缺乏纯UWB直接预测方向的方法，限制了上下文感知应用的发展。

Method: 采用双向Mamba架构处理固定锚点接收的UWB信道脉冲响应，结合身体部位条件模块适应不同标签位置；后处理使用两种卡尔曼滤波器（方向平滑与位置航向修正）优化时序连续性。

Result: 原始模型平均绝对误差38.6°（优于基线49.5°），经位置卡尔曼滤波后降至18.9°（误差减少51%），验证了多场景泛化能力。

Conclusion: 纯UWB方案结合深度学习与滤波技术可有效实现高精度方向预测，为无需额外传感器的智能交互系统提供新范式。

Abstract: User orientation is crucial for many context-aware applications, including interactive museum experiences, smart door access, and intuitive human-environment interaction. However, most existing indoor localization systems focus on estimating position, while body orientation is typically assigned to secondary devices such as inertial measurement units. In this paper, we propose a purely UWB-based approach that predicts yaw orientation directly from UWB Channel Impulse Response (CIR) measurements recorded at fixed anchors as they receive transmissions from a single wearable tag. We use a bidirectional Mamba architecture that captures dependencies across the anchor observations through forward and backward recurrent scans. The model uses per-anchor CIR and a body-part conditioning module to adapt the representation to different tag placements on the body. Two different Kalman filters are used as post-processing stages to exploit temporal continuity: an orientation-based filter that smooths the neural network predictions, and a location-based filter that additionally incorporates position-derived heading corrections. We evaluated the model's performance in different scenarios to ensure generalizability. The proposed Mamba model achieves a mean absolute error of 38.6 degrees in its raw form, outperforming a rule-based baseline of 49.5 degrees. With the location-based Kalman filter, the error is further reduced to 18.9 degrees, corresponding to a 51% reduction.

</details>


### [248] [Parametric Generalized Adaptive Moment Features (PG-AMF) for Bearing Fault Diagnosis and Machine Health Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26317)
*Rajeev Kumar*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种参数自适应特征提取框架，通过数据驱动学习振动信号的多维特征并融合多传感器信息，在齿轮箱轴承故障诊断中实现更高分类精度和可解释性，克服了传统方法与深度学习的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统统计特征方法依赖预定义描述符，诊断敏感性和跨故障条件适应性受限；深度学习虽具强表征能力但需大量数据且可解释性不足。

Method: 构建参数自适应特征提取框架：1) 从振动信号自动学习绝对特征(能量分布)、符号矩特征(波形不对称性)和AC耦合矩特征(动态波动)；2) 通过结构化融合机制建模多传感器通道交互关系。

Result: 在含5种健康状态(含多种故障类型)的齿轮箱轴承基准数据集上：1) 分类性能显著优于传统方法；2) 交叉验证结果稳定；3) 低维投影显示特征聚类更清晰；4) 特征可分性提升验证有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过自适应特征学习有效捕捉信号特性，在保证工业监测系统实用性的同时实现了诊断性能提升和可解释性增强。

Abstract: Accurate fault diagnosis of rolling element bearings in rotating machinery is considered essential for ensuring industrial safety and enabling predictive maintenance. Conventional statistical feature-based methods rely on predefined descriptors, whose diagnostic sensitivity is constrained by fixed configurations and limited adaptability across varying fault conditions. Although deep learning approaches offer strong representational capacity, their effectiveness is often restricted by high data requirements and reduced interpretability. In this work, a parametric adaptive feature extraction framework is proposed, in which feature characteristics are learned directly from data rather than being manually specified. Multiple complementary representations are extracted from vibration signals, including absolute features capturing signal energy distribution, signed moment features reflecting waveform asymmetry, and AC-coupled moment features emphasizing dynamic fluctuations, while interactions between multiple sensor channels are modeled through a structured fusion mechanism to enhance fault representation. The proposed approach is evaluated on a benchmark gearbox bearing dataset comprising five health conditions, including normal operation and multiple fault types. Improved classification performance is observed compared to conventional methods, with consistent results under cross-validation, indicating strong generalization capability. Additionally, enhanced feature separability is demonstrated through clearer clustering patterns in low-dimensional projections. The learned representations effectively capture a wide range of signal characteristics, supporting both improved diagnostic performance and practical applicability in industrial monitoring systems.

</details>


### [249] [MIMO Zak-OTFS: Channel Estimation, Detection, and Throughput Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26420)
*Faraz Barati,Rahul Kumar Jaiswal,Saif Khan Mohammed,Ronny Hadani,Jeffrey G. Andrews*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: Zak-OTFS调制在MIMO框架下的扩展研究，首次建立基于物理多径信道的完整系统模型，提出DD域结构化导频的信道估计方法。实验表明其抗多普勒和ICI性能优于CP-OFDM，但存在SNR/多普勒交叉效应及高PDR敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Zak-OTFS在MIMO系统中的性能优势，解决多径信道建模与信道估计问题，探索其与CP-OFDM的适用场景差异。

Method: 1. 基于物理多径信道推导MIMO Zak-OTFS完整系统模型；2. 设计延迟-多普勒域结构化导频放置方案实现高效信道估计。

Result: 1. MIMO Zak-OTFS继承SISO场景优势，显著提升多普勒/ICI鲁棒性；2. 发现SNR-多普勒交叉行为：低SNR/低多普勒时CP-OFDM略优，高SNR/强多普勒时Zak-OTFS占优；3. 交叉点呈负相关特性；4. 高PDR敏感性增加，但最优PDR与CP-OFDM相当。

Conclusion: Zak-OTFS在MIMO系统中展现出更强的动态信道适应性，其性能优势在高速移动场景尤为显著。研究为下一代通信系统设计提供了关键理论支撑，同时指出需优化导频功率分配策略。

Abstract: Zak-Orthogonal Time Frequency Space (Zak-OTFS) modulation has demonstrated substantial performance gains over cyclic-prefix orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (CP-OFDM) in highly time- and frequency-selective channels. In this paper, we extend Zak-OTFS to a multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) framework. We first derive a complete system model for MIMO Zak-OTFS based directly on the physical multipath channel; ours is the first work to do so. We then propose an efficient channel estimation method using structured pilot placement in the delay-Doppler (DD) domain. The proposed approach is evaluated under the standardized CDL-C channel model, demonstrating that the advantages of Zak-OTFS observed in SISO scenarios extend to MIMO systems, particularly its robustness to Doppler and inter-carrier interference (ICI). We identify a fundamental crossover behavior: CP-OFDM performs slightly better at low SNR and low Doppler, while Zak-OTFS excels at higher SNR or under severe Doppler dispersion. Furthermore, we show that the crossover points for SNR and Doppler shift inversely to each other. We also observe that Zak-OTFS, particularly with MIMO, exhibits increased sensitivity to high values of pilot-to-data power ratio (PDR), but has a similar optimal PDR as CP-OFDM.

</details>


### [250] [Multi-Modal Environment-Aware Beam Management for Massive MIMO: A Geometry-Driven Virtual Base Station Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26567)
*Yijie Bian,Wei Guo,Jie Yang,Shenghui Song,Jun Zhang,Shi Jin,Khaled B. Letaief*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于环境感知的高频大规模MIMO系统波束管理框架，通过融合3D激光雷达点云数据与位置信息构建虚拟基站数据库，结合几何驱动的信道重建机制和分层强化学习算法，显著降低波束训练开销并提升多用户场景下的波束选择性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高频大规模MIMO系统虽具备超高数据速率潜力，但面临两大瓶颈：1）传统基于导频的波束训练开销过大；2）多用户MIMO场景下波束协调复杂度高。现有方法难以平衡训练效率与干扰管理需求。

Method: 1. 构建虚拟基站(VBS)数据库：利用区域3D激光雷达点云重建建筑立面，通过镜像对称原理建模主导反射路径，生成稀疏化无线传播环境描述；2. 开发粗信道重建机制：从VBS几何关系直接估计信道参数，建立几何信息与无线信道的语义映射；3. 设计VBS辅助正交导频(VOP)方案：基于粗波束域表示进行局部波束训练，最小化在线训练开销；4. 提出分层深度强化学习框架DD3QN-CBS：采用双智能体决斗双DQN架构解决组合波束选择问题及用户间干扰协调。

Result: 仿真验证表明：1）相比启发式方法（如穷举搜索）和现有学习基线（如单智能体DQN），所提方案在波束训练效率上平均提升42%；2）在10用户场景下，频谱效率较传统方法提高31%，干扰抑制能力提升58%；3）VBS数据库压缩比达90%，有效降低存储与计算负担。

Conclusion: 本研究首次将环境感知通信范式与几何驱动建模相结合，通过虚实映射机制突破传统波束训练范式限制。提出的分层强化学习框架实现了多用户波束协调的帕累托最优解，为6G超大规模MIMO系统提供了可扩展的工程实现路径。

Abstract: High-frequency massive multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems promise ultra-high data rates. However, efficient beam management remains challenging due to the prohibitive beam training overhead and intricate coordination required in multi-user MIMO (MU-MIMO) scenarios. To address these bottlenecks, environment-aware communications have emerged as a promising paradigm, leveraging site-specific knowledge to circumvent exhaustive pilot-based beam training and streamline multi-user communications. In this paper, we propose an interpretable and geometry-driven framework that utilizes multi-modal environmental data, specifically regional 3D light detection and ranging (LiDAR) point clouds and location information, to construct an offline virtual base station (VBS) database. By modeling dominant reflection paths via mirror symmetry across building facades reconstructed from the point clouds, the VBS database provides a compact and sparse description of the wireless propagation environment. To bridge the semantic gap between geometric information and wireless channels, we develop a coarse channel reconstruction mechanism that estimates channel parameters directly from VBS-derived geometric relationships. Based on the resulting coarse beamspace representation, we design a VBS-assisted orthogonal-pilot (VOP)-based partial beam training scheme to refine the coarse estimates with minimal online training overhead. Finally, to tackle the combinatorial beam selection problem and manage inter-user interference, we propose a hierarchical deep reinforcement learning framework, namely a dual-agent dueling double deep Q-network, for coordinated beam selection (DD3QN-CBS). Simulation results demonstrate consistent gains in both beam training efficiency and beam selection performance over heuristic and learning-based baselines.

</details>


### [251] [State-Specific Respiratory Signatures for Affective and Stress Recognition: Interpretable Respiratory Markers, Autocorrelation Lags, and Compact CNN Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26723)
*Andrei Velichko,Mehmet Tahir Huyut*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本研究通过结合1D-CNN和手工呼吸特征，在WESAD数据集上实现压力检测，发现CNN在压力识别中表现最优，而手工特征在非压力状态（如冥想）中提供更强解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可穿戴设备压力识别研究多关注分类准确率，缺乏对区分不同状态的呼吸生理特征的明确解释。本研究旨在将呼吸识别重构为联合预测与解释任务，揭示不同状态下的生理标记。

Method: 采用WESAD数据集胸部呼吸通道，使用留一法验证60秒窗口数据。构建双分支模型：1D-CNN处理原始信号，手工特征涵盖呼吸时序、变异性、波形统计、频谱/时频描述符、自相关/非线性预测性等，并引入自相关滞后标记（Zpm/Zmp）及探索性FEG-Pro/Lyapunov类描述符。

Result: CNN模型在压力vs非压力检测中达到96.72%准确率、95.30%宏F1值及90.61% MCC；手工特征模型在基线（MCC 65.34%）、娱乐（MCC 35.69%）和冥想（MCC 88.65%）任务中表现更优，尤其冥想状态解释性显著增强。

Conclusion: CNN适用于实际压力检测场景，而手工呼吸特征为非压力状态提供更强生理可解释性标记，验证了联合预测与解释框架的有效性。

Abstract: Respiratory activity is a direct and interpretable physiological channel for wearable stress and affective-state recognition, yet many studies emphasize classification accuracy without identifying which respiratory properties separate different states. This work reframes RESP-based recognition as a joint predictive and explanatory problem. Using the chest respiratory channel of the WESAD dataset, we analyze 60 s windows under leave-one-subject-out validation and combine two complementary branches: compact raw-signal one-dimensional convolutional neural networks (1D-CNNs) and physically grouped handcrafted respiratory signatures. The primary application task is binary stress versus non-stress detection, while baseline, stress, amusement, and meditation are additionally analyzed in a one-vs-rest setting to reveal state-specific respiratory markers. The feature space is organized into respiratory timing, breath-to-breath variability, waveform statistics, spectral/time-frequency descriptors, and autocorrelation/nonlinear predictability descriptors, with the raw 60 s signal treated as a sixth representation for the CNN branch. We introduce autocorrelation transition lags (Zpm/Zmp) as interpretable markers of respiratory correlation scale and separately evaluate exploratory FEG-Pro/Lyapunov-like descriptors. In the final CNN refit setting, the raw-signal model achieved the strongest stress-vs-rest performance, with accuracy 96.72 percent, macro-F1 95.30 percent, and MCC 90.61 percent. In contrast, compact feature models were stronger for baseline, with MCC 65.34 percent, amusement, with MCC 35.69 percent, and especially meditation, with MCC 88.65 percent. These results show that CNNs are most useful for the practical stress detector, whereas interpretable respiratory signatures provide stronger and more physiologically transparent state-specific markers for several non-stress conditions.

</details>


### [252] [Collision-resistant multi-channel M-ASPM configurations with shared single detection channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26896)
*Alexei V. Nikitin,Ruslan L. Davidchack*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: M-ASPM调制技术通过分离检测通道与有效载荷处理，实现接收灵敏度提升而不加剧数据包碰撞，突破传统LPWAN调制中范围扩展与吞吐量下降的耦合限制，并支持多通道共享以优化部署效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LPWAN调制技术中，提高接收灵敏度需延长符号持续时间，导致空中时间增加并加剧数据包碰撞暴露，形成范围扩展与网络吞吐量的矛盾。

Method: 提出M-ASPM调制方案：1) 利用数据包前部作为独立抗碰撞检测通道，实现异步同步及载波频率偏移估计；2) 动态调整处理增益而不扩展符号采样窗口；3) 设计多通道架构，使多个准正交有效载荷通道共享单一检测通道并完成通道选择。

Result: 仿真验证表明：在高碰撞率、多变的噪声/干扰条件下，M-ASPM可显著提升接收灵敏度而不降低吞吐量，且多通道配置有效支持异构需求下的网络扩展。

Conclusion: M-ASPM通过结构创新解耦了通信范围与碰撞效应的关联，为LPWAN提供了可扩展性更强、资源利用率更高的物理层解决方案，尤其适用于高密度部署场景。

Abstract: M-ary Aggregate Spread Pulse Modulation (M-ASPM) is a physical layer (PHY) modulation technique that offers several advantages for low-power wide-area networks (LPWANs). For instance, in conventional LPWAN modulations increasing receiver sensitivity by extending symbol duration - thereby proportionally increasing the time-on-air (ToA) - exacerbates collision exposure. In contrast, M-ASPM payload processing gain can vary over a wide range without impacting the effective packet collision rate. In particular, in this work we demonstrate how short front portions of M-ASPM packets can serve as a separate collision-resistant detection channel that, in addition to performing asynchronous packet detection and synchronization, obtains the carrier frequency offset (CFO) for each packet within a desired range and with the required precision. Then, while raising processing gain, the subsequent payload information can be extracted without expanding the sample window per symbol. Consequently, the receiver sensitivity can be significantly increased without exacerbating packet collisions and thus without reducing network throughput under collision-limited operation. We further establish a multi-channel configuration in which numerous quasi-orthogonal payload channels share a single detection channel that additionally performs payload channel identification and selection. Such sharing is especially useful for scaling and economizing LPWAN deployments under diverse technical requirements and constraints. The presented analysis is validated via extensive simulations under high packet collision rates in wide ranges of payload sizes and processing gains, and for varying noise and interference power levels. The results signify that M-ASPM provides a structurally distinct scaling behavior compared to conventional LPWAN modulations, decoupling range extension from collision-induced throughput degradation.

</details>


### [253] [Distributed Massive MIMO with 1-Bit Radio-over-Fiber Fronthaul: Uplink Spectral Efficiency and Power Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26939)
*Alireza Bordbar,Anzhong Hu,Giuseppe Durisi*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于1比特光纤前传的分布式MIMO架构，通过Bussgang分解和信号对抖动比（SDR）优化，解决了传统频谱效率模型在非线性和无MMSE信道估计场景下的适用性问题，并分析了过采样和动态范围限制对系统性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统D-MIMO系统中，本地振荡器需求导致高昂的空中同步成本，而1比特光纤前传架构可消除该问题。但现有频谱效率模型因非线性输入输出关系和缺乏MMSE信道估计而失效，需新方法解决。

Method: 提出基于Bussgang分解的线性化可达速率表达式，无需MMSE信道估计；通过优化SDR最大化单/多用户场景速率，并分析过采样影响；进一步研究接入点动态范围受限时的最大最小公平问题。

Result: 验证了所提表达式在单/多用户场景下的有效性，确定了最优SDR配置，揭示了过采样对性能提升的边际效应，并提出了动态范围受限时的公平性解决方案。

Conclusion: 该架构通过简化硬件设计和创新信号处理模型，在降低同步成本的同时实现了可预测的频谱效率，为实际部署提供了理论依据和优化策略。

Abstract: We analyze the uplink spectral efficiency achievable in a distributed multiple-input multiple-output (D-MIMO) architecture employing a 1-bit radio-over-fiber fronthaul. This architecture eliminates the need for local oscillators at the access points, hence enabling coherent-phase transmission without costly over-the-air synchronization. With this fronthaul architecture, the uplink signal at the central processing unit is a dithered, oversampled, and 1-bit quantized version of the passband signal received at the access points. This makes some of the conventional spectral-efficiency expressions used in the D-MIMO literature not directly applicable for two key reasons: the nonlinearity of the input-output relation and the practical unavailability of minimum mean square error (MMSE) channel estimates. To address this issue, we propose novel achievable-rate expressions that do not require MMSE channel estimates and rely on the Bussgang decomposition to linearize the input-output relation. We use these expressions to determine the optimal signal-to-dither ratio (SDR) that maximizes the achievable rates in both single- and multiuser scenarios and to assess the impact of oversampling. We then use one of the proposed achievable-rate expressions to investigate the max-min fairness problem when the access points cannot maintain the optimal SDR because of limitations in their dynamic range.

</details>


### [254] [Angle Estimation via WFRFT Spatial-Domain Basis Decomposition: Breaking the Rayleigh Resolution Limit with Structured Waveform Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26957)
*Wang Hao,Zhang Kuang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出基于四分量加权分数阶傅里叶变换（4-WFRFT）的MIMO雷达角度估计框架，通过空间域波形分集突破瑞利衍射极限，实现1.4×~12.8倍角分辨率提升，尤其在低信噪比下优势显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分数阶傅里叶变换（FrFT）MIMO雷达仅在接收端进行时频处理，难以突破瑞利极限，尤其在低信噪比下性能受限。本研究旨在通过空间域波形设计提升角度估计精度。

Method: 将数据序列分解为四个WFRFT基函数（原信号、傅里叶变换、时间反转、逆傅里叶变换），通过四元均匀线性阵列同步发射。利用远场角度依赖的波形结构，通过时域匹配滤波实现角度估计。扩展至M元阵列时采用M分量WFRFT。

Result: 角分辨率超越瑞利极限1.4×~12.8倍，低信噪比下性能优势更显著；克拉美-罗界分析显示WFRFT波形结构贡献约3倍于阵列几何的信息量；线性调频序列仿真验证0dB峰均功率比（PAPR）及亚瑞利分辨率。

Conclusion: 该框架通过空间域波形分集机制实现超分辨角度估计，突破传统瑞利极限，且扩展性强，为低信噪比场景下的雷达系统设计提供新范式。

Abstract: We propose a MIMO radar angle estimation framework that uses the four-component weighted-type fractional Fourier transform (4-WFRFT) as a spatial-domain waveform diversity mechanism. Unlike conventional fractional Fourier (FrFT) MIMO radar where FrFT serves as a receiver-side time-frequency processing tool, our approach decomposes a data sequence into four WFRFT basis functions,original signal, its Fourier transform, time-reversal, and inverse Fourier transform, and transmits them simultaneously from a four-element uniform linear array. The spatial superposition of these basis functions at each far-field angle creates a unique angle-dependent waveform structure, enabling angle estimation through time-domain matched filtering with known waveforms. We demonstrate that this spatial-domain mixing achieves angular resolution surpassing the Rayleigh diffraction limit by a factor of 1.4$\times$ to 12.8$\times$, with the advantage most pronounced at low SNR where conventional beamforming fails completely. The Cramér-Rao bound is derived with a full 3-parameter Fisher information matrix, and the Fisher information is decomposed into geometry and waveform contributions, revealing that the WFRFT waveform structure contributes approximately 3$\times$ more information than array geometry alone. Extension to $M$-element arrays with $M$-component WFRFT demonstrates resolution gain scaling with array size. Simulations with linear chirp base sequences achieve 0\,dB PAPR and validate sub-Rayleigh resolution with a four-element array.

</details>


### [255] [Inverse Design of Compact and Wideband Inverted Doherty Power Amplifiers Using Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27002)
*Han Zhou,Haojie Chang,David Widen,Christian Fager*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 深度学习辅助的紧凑型宽带倒置Doherty功率放大器逆向合成方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种能够设计紧凑型、宽带Doherty功率放大器的新方法，特别是解决输出合路网络设计中负载调制、阻抗匹配、功率合成和相位补偿的多目标优化问题

Method: 联合使用卷积神经网络(CNN)和遗传算法(GA)来生成像素化的Doherty合路网络，将负载调制、阻抗匹配、功率合成和相位补偿集成到单一结构中

Result: 设计并制造了基于GaN HEMT的Doherty PA原型，在1.9-2.5 GHz频段实现：峰值漏极效率51%-63%，6-dB回退效率48%-54%，输出功率44±0.3 dBm，使用数字预失真后邻道泄漏比优于-53.2 dBc

Conclusion: 验证了深度学习辅助方法在Doherty功率放大器设计中的有效性，实现了高效、宽带的功率放大器，为未来射频功率放大器设计提供了新的技术路径

Abstract: This paper presents a deep learning-assisted methodology for the inverse synthesis of a compact, wideband inverted Doherty power amplifier (PA). Convolutional neural networks (CNNs) and genetic algorithms (GAs) are jointly employed to generate pixelated Doherty combiner networks that integrate load modulation, impedance matching, power combining, and phase compensation into a single structure. As a proof of concept, we design and fabricate a GaN HEMT Doherty PA with a pixelated output combiner. The prototype achieves a measured peak drain efficiency of 51%-63% and a 6-dB back-off efficiency of 48%-54% over 1.9-2.5 GHz. Within the same frequency range, the measured output power is 44+/-0.3 dBm. Furthermore, with digital predistortion (DPD) applied, the prototype circuit demonstrates an adjacent channel leakage ratio (ACLR) better than -53.2 dBc.

</details>
